# Graph Report - betting-model  (2026-11-27)

## Corpus Check
- Large corpus: 2921 files · ~5,211,281 words. Semantic extraction will be expensive (many Claude tokens). Consider running on a subfolder.

## Summary
- 59901 nodes · 148704 edges · 1233 communities (1042 shown, 191 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4203 edges (avg confidence: 0.86)
- Token cost: 7,320,213 input · 0 output
